### 词群 03.01｜研究者怎样分类动物 {#group-03-01}

**串联方式：** 从研究者的专业进入哺乳、爬行、两栖与脊椎动物，再按食性、物种和亚群分类；flora 作为植物群对照。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">biologist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-001" aria-label="定位 biologist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-001" data-mark-label="biologist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="biologist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="biologist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 biologist" title="在有道查询 biologist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 生物学家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>biologist</strong></a> led a zoologist, an ecologist and a botanist in surveying the island.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位生物学家带领动物学家、生态学家和植物学家调查该岛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The zoologist counted animals, the botanist identified plants, and the ecologist helped the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>biologist</strong></a> interpret their interactions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">动物学家清点动物，植物学家辨认植物，生态学家帮助生物学家解读它们的相互关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>biologist</strong></a> asked a botanist about the vegetation, a zoologist about the fauna and an ecologist about the ecosystem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">生物学家向植物学家询问植被、向动物学家询问动物群，并向生态学家询问生态系统。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a marine biologist</span> · 海洋生物学家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fieldwork&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fieldwork</a></span> · 野外研究</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biodiversity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biodiversity</a></span> · 生物多样性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A biologist led a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=zoologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>zoologist</strong></a>, an ecologist and a botanist in surveying the island.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位生物学家带领动物学家、生态学家和植物学家调查该岛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=zoologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>zoologist</strong></a> counted animals, the botanist identified plants, and the ecologist helped the biologist interpret their interactions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">动物学家清点动物，植物学家辨认植物，生态学家帮助生物学家解读它们的相互关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The biologist asked a botanist about the vegetation, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=zoologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>zoologist</strong></a> about the fauna and an ecologist about the ecosystem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">生物学家向植物学家询问植被、向动物学家询问动物群，并向生态学家询问生态系统。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A biologist led a zoologist, an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ecologist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ecologist</strong></a> and a botanist in surveying the island.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位生物学家带领动物学家、生态学家和植物学家调查该岛。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-005">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-005" class="vocabulary-word-title">mammal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-005" aria-label="定位 mammal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-005" data-mark-label="mammal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mammal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mammal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mammal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mammal" title="在有道查询 mammal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-005</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 哺乳动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every primate is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mammal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mammal</strong></a>, and every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mammal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mammal</strong></a> is a vertebrate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每种灵长类动物都是哺乳动物，每种哺乳动物都是脊椎动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit explains why a primate belongs to both the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mammal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mammal</strong></a> group and the wider vertebrate group.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览解释了为何灵长类动物既属于哺乳动物，也属于范围更大的脊椎动物类群。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A vertebrate need not be a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mammal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mammal</strong></a>, just as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mammal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mammal</strong></a> need not be a primate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">脊椎动物不一定是哺乳动物，正如哺乳动物不一定是灵长类动物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a marine mammal</span> · 海洋哺乳动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=backbone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">backbone</a></span> · 脊柱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">classification</a></span> · 分类</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-006">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-006" class="vocabulary-word-title">primate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-006" aria-label="定位 primate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-006" data-mark-label="primate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="primate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="primate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=primate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 primate" title="在有道查询 primate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-006</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 灵长目动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=primate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>primate</strong></a> is a mammal, and every mammal is a vertebrate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每种灵长类动物都是哺乳动物，每种哺乳动物都是脊椎动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit explains why a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=primate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>primate</strong></a> belongs to both the mammal group and the wider vertebrate group.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览解释了为何灵长类动物既属于哺乳动物，也属于范围更大的脊椎动物类群。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A vertebrate need not be a mammal, just as a mammal need not be a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=primate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>primate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">脊椎动物不一定是哺乳动物，正如哺乳动物不一定是灵长类动物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a non-human primate</span> · 非人类灵长类动物</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-007">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-007" class="vocabulary-word-title">vertebrate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-007" aria-label="定位 vertebrate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-007" data-mark-label="vertebrate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vertebrate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vertebrate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vertebrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vertebrate" title="在有道查询 vertebrate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-007</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 脊椎动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every primate is a mammal, and every mammal is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vertebrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vertebrate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每种灵长类动物都是哺乳动物，每种哺乳动物都是脊椎动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit explains why a primate belongs to both the mammal group and the wider <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vertebrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vertebrate</strong></a> group.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览解释了为何灵长类动物既属于哺乳动物，也属于范围更大的脊椎动物类群。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vertebrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vertebrate</strong></a> need not be a mammal, just as a mammal need not be a primate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">脊椎动物不一定是哺乳动物，正如哺乳动物不一定是灵长类动物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a terrestrial vertebrate</span> · 陆生脊椎动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=backbone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">backbone</a></span> · 脊柱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">classification</a></span> · 分类</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">reptile<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-008" aria-label="定位 reptile">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-008" data-mark-label="reptile" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reptile：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reptile：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reptile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reptile" title="在有道查询 reptile">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 爬行动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey classifies each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reptile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reptile</strong></a> and amphibian by diet, distinguishing a carnivore from a herbivore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查按食性为每种爬行动物和两栖动物分类，区分食肉动物与食草动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reptile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reptile</strong></a> may be a carnivore or a herbivore, while a young amphibian may change its diet as it grows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">爬行动物可能食肉或食草，幼年两栖动物的食性可能随成长而改变。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper prepared food for a carnivore, a herbivore, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reptile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reptile</strong></a> and an amphibian in separate enclosures.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员为分养的食肉动物、食草动物、爬行动物和两栖动物准备食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cold-blooded reptile</span> · 冷血爬行动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">predation</a></span> · 捕食</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diet</a></span> · 食性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-009">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-009" class="vocabulary-word-title">amphibian<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-009" aria-label="定位 amphibian">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-009" data-mark-label="amphibian" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="amphibian：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="amphibian：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amphibian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 amphibian" title="在有道查询 amphibian">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-009</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 两栖动物；水陆两用车；adj. 两栖动物的；水陆两用的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey classifies each reptile and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amphibian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amphibian</strong></a> by diet, distinguishing a carnivore from a herbivore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查按食性为每种爬行动物和两栖动物分类，区分食肉动物与食草动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reptile may be a carnivore or a herbivore, while a young <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amphibian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amphibian</strong></a> may change its diet as it grows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">爬行动物可能食肉或食草，幼年两栖动物的食性可能随成长而改变。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper prepared food for a carnivore, a herbivore, a reptile and an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amphibian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amphibian</strong></a> in separate enclosures.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员为分养的食肉动物、食草动物、爬行动物和两栖动物准备食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an aquatic amphibian</span> · 水生两栖动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">predation</a></span> · 捕食</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diet</a></span> · 食性</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">carnivore<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-010" aria-label="定位 carnivore">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-010" data-mark-label="carnivore" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="carnivore：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="carnivore：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carnivore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 carnivore" title="在有道查询 carnivore">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 食肉动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey classifies each reptile and amphibian by diet, distinguishing a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carnivore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carnivore</strong></a> from a herbivore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查按食性为每种爬行动物和两栖动物分类，区分食肉动物与食草动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reptile may be a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carnivore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carnivore</strong></a> or a herbivore, while a young amphibian may change its diet as it grows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">爬行动物可能食肉或食草，幼年两栖动物的食性可能随成长而改变。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper prepared food for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carnivore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carnivore</strong></a>, a herbivore, a reptile and an amphibian in separate enclosures.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员为分养的食肉动物、食草动物、爬行动物和两栖动物准备食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a large carnivore</span> · 大型食肉动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">predation</a></span> · 捕食</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diet</a></span> · 食性</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-011">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 食草动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey classifies each reptile and amphibian by diet, distinguishing a carnivore from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=herbivore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>herbivore</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查按食性为每种爬行动物和两栖动物分类，区分食肉动物与食草动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reptile may be a carnivore or a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=herbivore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>herbivore</strong></a>, while a young amphibian may change its diet as it grows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">爬行动物可能食肉或食草，幼年两栖动物的食性可能随成长而改变。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper prepared food for a carnivore, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=herbivore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>herbivore</strong></a>, a reptile and an amphibian in separate enclosures.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员为分养的食肉动物、食草动物、爬行动物和两栖动物准备食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a grazing herbivore</span> · 食草动物</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-012">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-012" class="vocabulary-word-title">creature<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-012" aria-label="定位 creature">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-012" data-mark-label="creature" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="creature：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="creature：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=creature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 creature" title="在有道查询 creature">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-012</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 生物，动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting wildlife means conserving flora and fauna, including every threatened species and the smallest <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=creature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>creature</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护野生生物意味着保护植物群和动物群，包括每个受威胁物种和最微小的生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wildlife survey recorded flora and fauna and identified one tiny <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=creature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>creature</strong></a> as a previously unknown species.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">野生生物调查记录了植物群和动物群，并确定一种小生物是此前未知的物种。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=creature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>creature</strong></a> cannot survive in isolation, so species protection considers the surrounding wildlife, flora and fauna.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">生物无法孤立生存，因此物种保护须考虑周围的野生生物及动植物群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an endangered creature</span> · 濒危生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biodiversity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biodiversity</a></span> · 生物多样性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">wildlife<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-013" aria-label="定位 wildlife">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-013" data-mark-label="wildlife" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wildlife：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wildlife：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wildlife&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wildlife" title="在有道查询 wildlife">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 野生动物；野生生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wildlife&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wildlife</strong></a> means conserving flora and fauna, including every threatened species and the smallest creature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护野生生物意味着保护植物群和动物群，包括每个受威胁物种和最微小的生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wildlife&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wildlife</strong></a> survey recorded flora and fauna and identified one tiny creature as a previously unknown species.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">野生生物调查记录了植物群和动物群，并确定一种小生物是此前未知的物种。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A creature cannot survive in isolation, so species protection considers the surrounding <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wildlife&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wildlife</strong></a>, flora and fauna.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">生物无法孤立生存，因此物种保护须考虑周围的野生生物及动植物群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">protect wildlife</span> · 保护野生生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biodiversity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biodiversity</a></span> · 生物多样性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">fauna<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-014" aria-label="定位 fauna">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-014" data-mark-label="fauna" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fauna：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fauna：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fauna&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fauna" title="在有道查询 fauna">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(尤指某一地区的)动物群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting wildlife means conserving flora and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fauna&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fauna</strong></a>, including every threatened species and the smallest creature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护野生生物意味着保护植物群和动物群，包括每个受威胁物种和最微小的生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wildlife survey recorded flora and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fauna&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fauna</strong></a> and identified one tiny creature as a previously unknown species.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">野生生物调查记录了植物群和动物群，并确定一种小生物是此前未知的物种。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A creature cannot survive in isolation, so species protection considers the surrounding wildlife, flora and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fauna&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fauna</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">生物无法孤立生存，因此物种保护须考虑周围的野生生物及动植物群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">native fauna</span> · 本土动物群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biodiversity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biodiversity</a></span> · 生物多样性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">flora<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-015" aria-label="定位 flora">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-015" data-mark-label="flora" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="flora：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="flora：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flora&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 flora" title="在有道查询 flora">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(尤指某一地区的)植物群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting wildlife means conserving <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flora&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flora</strong></a> and fauna, including every threatened species and the smallest creature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护野生生物意味着保护植物群和动物群，包括每个受威胁物种和最微小的生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wildlife survey recorded <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flora&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flora</strong></a> and fauna and identified one tiny creature as a previously unknown species.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">野生生物调查记录了植物群和动物群，并确定一种小生物是此前未知的物种。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A creature cannot survive in isolation, so species protection considers the surrounding wildlife, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flora&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flora</strong></a> and fauna.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">生物无法孤立生存，因此物种保护须考虑周围的野生生物及动植物群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">native flora</span> · 本土植物群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biodiversity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biodiversity</a></span> · 生物多样性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-016">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-016" class="vocabulary-word-title">species<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-016" aria-label="定位 species">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-016" data-mark-label="species" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="species：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="species：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=species&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 species" title="在有道查询 species">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-016</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [ 单复数同形 ] 种，物种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting wildlife means conserving flora and fauna, including every threatened <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=species&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>species</strong></a> and the smallest creature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护野生生物意味着保护植物群和动物群，包括每个受威胁物种和最微小的生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wildlife survey recorded flora and fauna and identified one tiny creature as a previously unknown <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=species&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>species</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">野生生物调查记录了植物群和动物群，并确定一种小生物是此前未知的物种。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A creature cannot survive in isolation, so <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=species&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>species</strong></a> protection considers the surrounding wildlife, flora and fauna.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">生物无法孤立生存，因此物种保护须考虑周围的野生生物及动植物群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an endangered species</span> · 濒危物种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=biodiversity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">biodiversity</a></span> · 生物多样性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-031">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-031" class="vocabulary-word-title">subgroup<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-031" aria-label="定位 subgroup">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-031" data-mark-label="subgroup" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="subgroup：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="subgroup：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subgroup&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 subgroup" title="在有道查询 subgroup">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-031</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 子群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Genetic evidence identifies this population as a distinct <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subgroup&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subgroup</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗传证据确定这个种群是一个独特亚群。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subgroup&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subgroup</strong></a> feeds mainly at night.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个亚群主要在夜间觅食。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared the behaviour of each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subgroup&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subgroup</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较了各亚群的行为。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a distinct subgroup</span> · 独特的亚群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">classification</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">population</a></span> · 种群</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 03.02｜群体与野兽形象 {#group-03-02}

**串联方式：** 用鸟群、兽群和虫群描述聚集，再对比人群与带有评价色彩的野兽称呼。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-017">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-017" class="vocabulary-word-title">flock<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-017" aria-label="定位 flock">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-017" data-mark-label="flock" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="flock：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="flock：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 flock" title="在有道查询 flock">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-017</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(羊或鸟)群；v. 聚集</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flock</strong></a> of birds flew above a herd of cattle as a swarm of bees crossed the field.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一群鸟从牛群上空飞过，一群蜜蜂同时穿过田野。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer moved the herd away from a swarm of bees while a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flock</strong></a> settled nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民将牛群赶离蜂群，一群鸟则在附近落下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wetland supports a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flock</strong></a> of geese, a grazing herd and a seasonal swarm of insects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">湿地供养着一群鹅、一群食草动物和季节性聚集的昆虫群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a flock of birds</span> · 一群鸟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=migration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">migration</a></span> · 迁徙</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggregation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aggregation</a></span> · 聚集</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-018">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-018" class="vocabulary-word-title">herd<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-018" aria-label="定位 herd">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-018" data-mark-label="herd" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="herd：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="herd：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=herd&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 herd" title="在有道查询 herd">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-018</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 兽群，畜群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flock of birds flew above a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=herd&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>herd</strong></a> of cattle as a swarm of bees crossed the field.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一群鸟从牛群上空飞过，一群蜜蜂同时穿过田野。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer moved the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=herd&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>herd</strong></a> away from a swarm of bees while a flock settled nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民将牛群赶离蜂群，一群鸟则在附近落下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wetland supports a flock of geese, a grazing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=herd&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>herd</strong></a> and a seasonal swarm of insects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">湿地供养着一群鹅、一群食草动物和季节性聚集的昆虫群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a herd of cattle</span> · 一群牛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=migration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">migration</a></span> · 迁徙</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggregation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aggregation</a></span> · 聚集</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">swarm<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-019" aria-label="定位 swarm">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-019" data-mark-label="swarm" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="swarm：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="swarm：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swarm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 swarm" title="在有道查询 swarm">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(蜜蜂等昆虫的)一大群；(迅速移动的)人群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flock of birds flew above a herd of cattle as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swarm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swarm</strong></a> of bees crossed the field.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一群鸟从牛群上空飞过，一群蜜蜂同时穿过田野。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer moved the herd away from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swarm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swarm</strong></a> of bees while a flock settled nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民将牛群赶离蜂群，一群鸟则在附近落下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wetland supports a flock of geese, a grazing herd and a seasonal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swarm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swarm</strong></a> of insects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">湿地供养着一群鹅、一群食草动物和季节性聚集的昆虫群。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a swarm of bees</span> · 一群蜜蜂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=migration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">migration</a></span> · 迁徙</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggregation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aggregation</a></span> · 聚集</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-020">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-020" class="vocabulary-word-title">throng<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-020" aria-label="定位 throng">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-020" data-mark-label="throng" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="throng：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="throng：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=throng&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 throng" title="在有道查询 throng">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-020</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 人群，一大群人；v. 群集</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A crowd joined the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=throng&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>throng</strong></a> around the cage, condemning the brute for his cruel treatment of the beast.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一群人加入笼子周围的人群，谴责那个暴徒残酷对待野兽。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crowd watched a cruel brute drag the beast through a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=throng&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>throng</strong></a> of frightened spectators.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人群看着一个残暴的人拖着野兽穿过惊恐的观众群。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=throng&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>throng</strong></a> gathered after the crowd heard that a brute had been arrested for being cruel to a captive beast.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人群听说一名暴徒因残酷对待圈养野兽被捕后，更多人聚集过来。</p></li>
</ol>
