### 词群 03.03｜追溯物种来源 {#group-03-03}

**串联方式：** 从起源和祖先一路追到后代，分别掌握 derive from、originate in、stem from 的结构。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-025">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-025" class="vocabulary-word-title">derive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-025" aria-label="定位 derive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-025" data-mark-label="derive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="derive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="derive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=derive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 derive" title="在有道查询 derive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-025</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 得到；(使)源自</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">These traits <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=derive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>derive</strong></a> from inherited changes, originate in earlier populations and stem from evolutionary processes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些特征源自遗传变化、起于早期种群，并来自演化过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Differences that stem from habitat may originate locally rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=derive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>derive</strong></a> from separate ancestry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由栖息地造成的差异可能在本地产生，而非来自不同祖系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists ask whether these features <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=derive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>derive</strong></a> from a common ancestor, originate independently or stem from similar environments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家探究这些特征是源自共同祖先、独立产生，还是来自相似环境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">derive from an ancestor</span> · 源自某祖先</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=origin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">origin</a></span> · 起源</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ancestry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ancestry</a></span> · 祖系</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-026">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-026" class="vocabulary-word-title">originate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-026" aria-label="定位 originate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-026" data-mark-label="originate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="originate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="originate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=originate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 originate" title="在有道查询 originate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-026</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 发源，来自；创始</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">These traits derive from inherited changes, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=originate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>originate</strong></a> in earlier populations and stem from evolutionary processes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些特征源自遗传变化、起于早期种群，并来自演化过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Differences that stem from habitat may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=originate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>originate</strong></a> locally rather than derive from separate ancestry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由栖息地造成的差异可能在本地产生，而非来自不同祖系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists ask whether these features derive from a common ancestor, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=originate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>originate</strong></a> independently or stem from similar environments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家探究这些特征是源自共同祖先、独立产生，还是来自相似环境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">originate in Africa</span> · 起源于非洲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=origin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">origin</a></span> · 起源</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ancestry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ancestry</a></span> · 祖系</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-027">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-027" class="vocabulary-word-title">stem<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-027" aria-label="定位 stem">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-027" data-mark-label="stem" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stem：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stem：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stem" title="在有道查询 stem">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-027</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 起源于，来自；n. 茎，梗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">These traits derive from inherited changes, originate in earlier populations and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stem</strong></a> from evolutionary processes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些特征源自遗传变化、起于早期种群，并来自演化过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Differences that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stem</strong></a> from habitat may originate locally rather than derive from separate ancestry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由栖息地造成的差异可能在本地产生，而非来自不同祖系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists ask whether these features derive from a common ancestor, originate independently or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stem</strong></a> from similar environments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家探究这些特征是源自共同祖先、独立产生，还是来自相似环境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stem from a change</span> · 源于变化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=origin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">origin</a></span> · 起源</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ancestry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ancestry</a></span> · 祖系</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-028">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-028" class="vocabulary-word-title">ancestor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-028" aria-label="定位 ancestor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-028" data-mark-label="ancestor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ancestor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ancestor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ancestor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ancestor" title="在有道查询 ancestor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-028</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 祖宗，祖先</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each descendant carries genetic material passed from an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ancestor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ancestor</strong></a> through generations of offspring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每个后代都带有祖先通过一代代子代传下的遗传物质。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The offspring of this rare bird may become the last living descendant of a once-common <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ancestor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ancestor</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种珍稀鸟的子代可能成为一个曾经常见祖先的最后存活后裔。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared an ancient <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ancestor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ancestor</strong></a> with a modern descendant and its offspring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较了一种古代祖先、现代后裔及其子代。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a common ancestor</span> · 共同祖先</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 遗传</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lineage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lineage</a></span> · 血统</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">descendant<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-029" aria-label="定位 descendant">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-029" data-mark-label="descendant" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="descendant：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="descendant：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=descendant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 descendant" title="在有道查询 descendant">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 后裔，后代；adj. 下降的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=descendant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>descendant</strong></a> carries genetic material passed from an ancestor through generations of offspring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每个后代都带有祖先通过一代代子代传下的遗传物质。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The offspring of this rare bird may become the last living <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=descendant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>descendant</strong></a> of a once-common ancestor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种珍稀鸟的子代可能成为一个曾经常见祖先的最后存活后裔。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared an ancient ancestor with a modern <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=descendant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>descendant</strong></a> and its offspring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较了一种古代祖先、现代后裔及其子代。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a direct descendant</span> · 直系后代</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 遗传</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lineage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lineage</a></span> · 血统</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-030">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-030" class="vocabulary-word-title">offspring<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-030" aria-label="定位 offspring">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-030" data-mark-label="offspring" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="offspring：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="offspring：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offspring&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 offspring" title="在有道查询 offspring">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-030</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [ 单复数同形 ] 后代，子孙；产物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each descendant carries genetic material passed from an ancestor through generations of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offspring&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offspring</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每个后代都带有祖先通过一代代子代传下的遗传物质。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offspring&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offspring</strong></a> of this rare bird may become the last living descendant of a once-common ancestor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种珍稀鸟的子代可能成为一个曾经常见祖先的最后存活后裔。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared an ancient ancestor with a modern descendant and its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offspring&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offspring</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较了一种古代祖先、现代后裔及其子代。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">healthy offspring</span> · 健康后代</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 遗传</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lineage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lineage</a></span> · 血统</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 03.04｜繁殖与成长 {#group-03-04}

**串联方式：** 从进食、求偶和交配，走到产卵、孵化与成熟；杂交、繁殖能力和不育是扩展维度。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-032">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-032" class="vocabulary-word-title">feed<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-032" aria-label="定位 feed">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-032" data-mark-label="feed" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="feed：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="feed：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 feed" title="在有道查询 feed">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-032</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 供养；喂，饲养；进食</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Breeders <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>feed</strong></a> the animals carefully, breed selected lines and test whether those lines interbreed or hybridise successfully.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">育种者精心喂养动物，繁育选定品系，并测试这些品系能否成功交配杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To breed healthy animals, farmers must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>feed</strong></a> them well and understand which populations can interbreed or hybridise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要繁育健康动物，农民必须良好喂养，并了解哪些种群能够交配或杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>feed</strong></a> captive fish, breed them under controlled conditions and observe whether related species interbreed and hybridise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员喂养圈养鱼类，在受控条件下繁育，并观察近缘物种是否交配杂交。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">feed livestock</span> · 喂养牲畜</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">breeding</a></span> · 繁育</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybrid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hybrid</a></span> · 杂种</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">breed<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-033" aria-label="定位 breed">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-033" data-mark-label="breed" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="breed：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="breed：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 breed" title="在有道查询 breed">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 饲养；繁殖；n. 品种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Breeders feed the animals carefully, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breed</strong></a> selected lines and test whether those lines interbreed or hybridise successfully.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">育种者精心喂养动物，繁育选定品系，并测试这些品系能否成功交配杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breed</strong></a> healthy animals, farmers must feed them well and understand which populations can interbreed or hybridise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要繁育健康动物，农民必须良好喂养，并了解哪些种群能够交配或杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers feed captive fish, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breed</strong></a> them under controlled conditions and observe whether related species interbreed and hybridise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员喂养圈养鱼类，在受控条件下繁育，并观察近缘物种是否交配杂交。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">breed cattle</span> · 繁育牛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">breeding</a></span> · 繁育</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybrid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hybrid</a></span> · 杂种</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">interbreed<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-034" aria-label="定位 interbreed">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-034" data-mark-label="interbreed" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="interbreed：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="interbreed：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interbreed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 interbreed" title="在有道查询 interbreed">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)品种间杂交，(使)生混种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Breeders feed the animals carefully, breed selected lines and test whether those lines <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interbreed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interbreed</strong></a> or hybridise successfully.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">育种者精心喂养动物，繁育选定品系，并测试这些品系能否成功交配杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To breed healthy animals, farmers must feed them well and understand which populations can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interbreed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interbreed</strong></a> or hybridise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要繁育健康动物，农民必须良好喂养，并了解哪些种群能够交配或杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers feed captive fish, breed them under controlled conditions and observe whether related species <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interbreed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interbreed</strong></a> and hybridise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员喂养圈养鱼类，在受控条件下繁育，并观察近缘物种是否交配杂交。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">interbreed successfully</span> · 成功杂交</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">breeding</a></span> · 繁育</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybrid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hybrid</a></span> · 杂种</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-035">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-035" class="vocabulary-word-title">hybridise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-035" aria-label="定位 hybridise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-035" data-mark-label="hybridise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hybridise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hybridise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hybridise" title="在有道查询 hybridise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-035</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(= hybridize)；v.(使)杂交</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Breeders feed the animals carefully, breed selected lines and test whether those lines interbreed or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hybridise</strong></a> successfully.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">育种者精心喂养动物，繁育选定品系，并测试这些品系能否成功交配杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To breed healthy animals, farmers must feed them well and understand which populations can interbreed or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hybridise</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要繁育健康动物，农民必须良好喂养，并了解哪些种群能够交配或杂交。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers feed captive fish, breed them under controlled conditions and observe whether related species interbreed and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hybridise</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员喂养圈养鱼类，在受控条件下繁育，并观察近缘物种是否交配杂交。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hybridise varieties</span> · 杂交品种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breeding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">breeding</a></span> · 繁育</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybrid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hybrid</a></span> · 杂种</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-036">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-036" class="vocabulary-word-title">proliferate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-036" aria-label="定位 proliferate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-036" data-mark-label="proliferate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="proliferate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="proliferate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proliferate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 proliferate" title="在有道查询 proliferate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-036</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 迅速增殖；剧增</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Courtship helps an animal find a mate, but sterility prevents its lineage from continuing to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proliferate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proliferate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">求偶帮助动物找到配偶，但不育会阻止其后代继续繁衍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The study examined how sterility and disrupted courtship affect populations that normally mate and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proliferate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proliferate</strong></a> quickly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究考察不育和求偶受干扰如何影响通常交配并快速繁衍的种群。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even where animals <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proliferate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proliferate</strong></a>, unsuccessful courtship or sterility may leave an individual unable to reproduce with a mate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使动物种群繁衍旺盛，求偶失败或不育也可能使个体无法与配偶繁殖。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">proliferate rapidly</span> · 迅速增殖</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fertility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fertility</a></span> · 生育力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproduction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reproduction</a></span> · 繁殖</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 产(卵)；放置；铺设</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Birds <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lay</strong></a> eggs that hatch into a brood, while fish spawn and their young mature in water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鸟产卵并孵出一窝雏鸟，而鱼在水中产卵，幼鱼也在水中成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students watched birds <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lay</strong></a> eggs, chicks hatch into a brood, and fish spawn before their young began to mature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生观察鸟产卵、雏鸟成窝孵出，以及鱼产卵后幼鱼开始成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows a brood from eggs birds <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lay</strong></a> and hatch, then shows fish spawn and mature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片先跟拍一窝鸟从产卵到孵化，再展示鱼的产卵及成长。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Birds lay eggs that hatch into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brood&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brood</strong></a>, while fish spawn and their young mature in water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鸟产卵并孵出一窝雏鸟，而鱼在水中产卵，幼鱼也在水中成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students watched birds lay eggs, chicks hatch into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brood&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brood</strong></a>, and fish spawn before their young began to mature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生观察鸟产卵、雏鸟成窝孵出，以及鱼产卵后幼鱼开始成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brood&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brood</strong></a> from eggs birds lay and hatch, then shows fish spawn and mature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片先跟拍一窝鸟从产卵到孵化，再展示鱼的产卵及成长。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a brood of chicks</span> · 一窝雏鸟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=incubation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">incubation</a></span> · 孵化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=juvenile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">juvenile</a></span> · 幼体</li></ul>

### 词群 03.05｜观察动物的身体 {#group-03-05}

**串联方式：** 按皮肤、爪、喙、鳍、翅、羽、毛及象鼻、象牙和角辨认外部结构。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-045">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-045" class="vocabulary-word-title">skin<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-045" aria-label="定位 skin">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-045" data-mark-label="skin" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="skin：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="skin：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 skin" title="在有道查询 skin">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-045</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 皮肤；外皮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vet examined the mammal's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>skin</strong></a>, paw and claw, the bird's beak and the fish's fin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">兽医检查了哺乳动物的皮肤、脚爪和爪尖、鸟的喙以及鱼的鳍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A claw helps grip, a paw supports movement, a beak handles food, a fin aids swimming, and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>skin</strong></a> provides protection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尖爪帮助抓握，脚爪支撑运动，喙处理食物，鳍帮助游泳，皮肤提供保护。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit compares a furry paw and claw with a scaly fin, a bird's beak and reptile <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>skin</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将长毛的脚爪和尖爪，与带鳞的鳍、鸟喙和爬行动物皮肤相比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">thick skin</span> · 厚皮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anatomy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">anatomy</a></span> · 解剖结构</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适应</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=claw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>claw</strong></a> helps grip, a paw supports movement, a beak handles food, a fin aids swimming, and skin provides protection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尖爪帮助抓握，脚爪支撑运动，喙处理食物，鳍帮助游泳，皮肤提供保护。</p></li>

<summary><h4 id="word-03-049" class="vocabulary-word-title">fin<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-049" aria-label="定位 fin">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-049" data-mark-label="fin" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fin：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fin：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fin" title="在有道查询 fin">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-049</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 鳍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vet examined the mammal's skin, paw and claw, the bird's beak and the fish's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fin</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">兽医检查了哺乳动物的皮肤、脚爪和爪尖、鸟的喙以及鱼的鳍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A claw helps grip, a paw supports movement, a beak handles food, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fin</strong></a> aids swimming, and skin provides protection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尖爪帮助抓握，脚爪支撑运动，喙处理食物，鳍帮助游泳，皮肤提供保护。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit compares a furry paw and claw with a scaly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fin</strong></a>, a bird's beak and reptile skin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将长毛的脚爪和尖爪，与带鳞的鳍、鸟喙和爬行动物皮肤相比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a dorsal fin</span> · 背鳍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anatomy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">anatomy</a></span> · 解剖结构</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适应</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 鬈发；卷曲；螺旋状物；v.(使)卷曲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sculpture shows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curl</strong></a> in the elephant's trunk beside carved ivory and a replica horn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雕塑展示了象鼻的卷曲，旁边是象牙雕刻和仿制兽角。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curl</strong></a> of the trunk appears in the painting, but neither ivory nor horn was used to frame it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">画中象鼻卷起，但画框既未使用象牙也未使用兽角。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum contrasts a ram's horn with ivory and shows how a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curl</strong></a> forms in an elephant's flexible trunk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆比较公羊角与象牙，并展示灵活的象鼻如何形成卷曲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a tight curl</span> · 紧卷</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sculpture shows a curl in the elephant's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trunk</strong></a> beside carved ivory and a replica horn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雕塑展示了象鼻的卷曲，旁边是象牙雕刻和仿制兽角。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A curl of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trunk</strong></a> appears in the painting, but neither ivory nor horn was used to frame it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">画中象鼻卷起，但画框既未使用象牙也未使用兽角。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum contrasts a ram's horn with ivory and shows how a curl forms in an elephant's flexible <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trunk</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆比较公羊角与象牙，并展示灵活的象鼻如何形成卷曲。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 03.06｜昆虫及小型无脊椎动物 {#group-03-06}

**串联方式：** 给常见小动物命名，再区分害虫和寄生者；蜘蛛和蠕虫不等同于昆虫。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">insect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-056" aria-label="定位 insect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-056" data-mark-label="insect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="insect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="insect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 insect" title="在有道查询 insect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 昆虫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>insect</strong></a> is a crop pest, while that worm is a parasite of livestock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种昆虫是作物害虫，而那种蠕虫寄生于牲畜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer distinguished a beneficial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>insect</strong></a> from a pest and a harmless worm from a parasite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民区分有益昆虫与害虫，也区分无害蠕虫与寄生虫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A parasite may be a worm or an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>insect</strong></a>, but not every small animal is a pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">寄生虫可能是蠕虫或昆虫，但并非每种小动物都是害虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an insect bite</span> · 昆虫叮咬</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=host&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">host</a></span> · 宿主</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infestation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">infestation</a></span> · 侵扰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">worm<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-057" aria-label="定位 worm">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-057" data-mark-label="worm" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="worm：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="worm：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=worm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 worm" title="在有道查询 worm">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 蠕虫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This insect is a crop pest, while that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=worm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>worm</strong></a> is a parasite of livestock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种昆虫是作物害虫，而那种蠕虫寄生于牲畜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer distinguished a beneficial insect from a pest and a harmless <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=worm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>worm</strong></a> from a parasite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民区分有益昆虫与害虫，也区分无害蠕虫与寄生虫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A parasite may be a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=worm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>worm</strong></a> or an insect, but not every small animal is a pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">寄生虫可能是蠕虫或昆虫，但并非每种小动物都是害虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an earth-dwelling worm</span> · 土栖蠕虫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=host&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">host</a></span> · 宿主</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infestation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">infestation</a></span> · 侵扰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">pest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-058" aria-label="定位 pest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-058" data-mark-label="pest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pest" title="在有道查询 pest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 害虫；有害动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This insect is a crop <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pest</strong></a>, while that worm is a parasite of livestock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种昆虫是作物害虫，而那种蠕虫寄生于牲畜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer distinguished a beneficial insect from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pest</strong></a> and a harmless worm from a parasite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民区分有益昆虫与害虫，也区分无害蠕虫与寄生虫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A parasite may be a worm or an insect, but not every small animal is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pest</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">寄生虫可能是蠕虫或昆虫，但并非每种小动物都是害虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an agricultural pest</span> · 农业害虫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=host&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">host</a></span> · 宿主</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infestation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">infestation</a></span> · 侵扰</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-059">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This insect is a crop pest, while that worm is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parasite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parasite</strong></a> of livestock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种昆虫是作物害虫，而那种蠕虫寄生于牲畜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer distinguished a beneficial insect from a pest and a harmless worm from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parasite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parasite</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民区分有益昆虫与害虫，也区分无害蠕虫与寄生虫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parasite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parasite</strong></a> may be a worm or an insect, but not every small animal is a pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">寄生虫可能是蠕虫或昆虫，但并非每种小动物都是害虫。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spider&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spider</strong></a> caught a mosquito while a butterfly rested near a chirping cricket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">蜘蛛捕住一只蚊子，一只蝴蝶则停在鸣叫的蟋蟀附近。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Children watched a butterfly land, a cricket jump and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spider&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spider</strong></a> trap a mosquito.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子们看蝴蝶降落、蟋蟀跳跃，以及蜘蛛捕住蚊子。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 03.07｜野生动物与栖息差异 {#group-03-07}

**串联方式：** 从极地、水域到陆地辨认代表动物；dragon 作为虚构动物单列记忆。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">penguin<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-064" aria-label="定位 penguin">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-064" data-mark-label="penguin" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="penguin：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="penguin：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penguin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 penguin" title="在有道查询 penguin">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 企鹅</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penguin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>penguin</strong></a> and a seal at sea before visiting a tortoise and a turtle on tropical islands.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片先跟拍海中的企鹅与海豹，再探访热带岛屿的陆龟与海龟。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike a tortoise, a sea turtle swims efficiently, as do a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penguin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>penguin</strong></a> and a seal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与陆龟不同，海龟善于游泳，企鹅和海豹也是如此。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rescue centre separated the injured turtle from the tortoise, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penguin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>penguin</strong></a> and seal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救护中心将受伤海龟与陆龟、企鹅及海豹分开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an emperor penguin</span> · 帝企鹅</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flipper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">flipper</a></span> · 鳍状肢</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coastline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coastline</a></span> · 海岸线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<summary><h4 id="word-03-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">turtle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-067" aria-label="定位 turtle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-067" data-mark-label="turtle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="turtle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="turtle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turtle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 turtle" title="在有道查询 turtle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 海龟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows a penguin and a seal at sea before visiting a tortoise and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turtle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turtle</strong></a> on tropical islands.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片先跟拍海中的企鹅与海豹，再探访热带岛屿的陆龟与海龟。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike a tortoise, a sea <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turtle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turtle</strong></a> swims efficiently, as do a penguin and a seal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与陆龟不同，海龟善于游泳，企鹅和海豹也是如此。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rescue centre separated the injured <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turtle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turtle</strong></a> from the tortoise, penguin and seal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救护中心将受伤海龟与陆龟、企鹅及海豹分开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sea turtle</span> · 海龟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flipper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">flipper</a></span> · 鳍状肢</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coastline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coastline</a></span> · 海岸线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-068">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 鲸鱼；v. 捕鲸；猛击</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whale</strong></a>, kangaroo, camel, panda and elephant are all mammals adapted to very different environments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鲸、袋鼠、骆驼、熊猫和象都是适应迥异环境的哺乳动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The programme compared how a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whale</strong></a> swims, a kangaroo hops, a camel walks, a panda climbs and an elephant travels.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">节目比较了鲸游泳、袋鼠跳跃、骆驼行走、熊猫攀爬和象迁移的方式。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Conservation funding supported a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whale</strong></a> sanctuary, a kangaroo reserve and breeding projects for the camel, panda and elephant.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护资金支持鲸庇护区、袋鼠保护区，以及骆驼、熊猫和象的繁育项目。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a blue whale</span> · 蓝鲸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mammal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mammal</a></span> · 哺乳动物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适应</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-076">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-076" class="vocabulary-word-title">bear<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-076" aria-label="定位 bear">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-076" data-mark-label="bear" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bear：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bear：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bear" title="在有道查询 bear">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-076</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 熊；v. 承受、承担；生育（其他语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tale features a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bear</strong></a>, a wolf and a fox who outwit a dragon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">故事讲述熊、狼和狐狸智胜巨龙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the imaginary dragon, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bear</strong></a>, wolf and fox in this story are real animals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与虚构的龙不同，故事中的熊、狼和狐狸是真实动物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A carved dragon guarded the entrance to an exhibit on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bear</strong></a>, wolf and fox in folklore.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一条雕刻的龙守在展览入口，该展览介绍民间传说中的熊、狼和狐狸。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 03.08｜幼崽、家畜与役用动物 {#group-03-08}

**串联方式：** 先记幼崽称呼，再看牛马驴等动物以及鞍、挽具；zebra 提供野生近亲对照。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">cub<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-080" aria-label="定位 cub">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-080" data-mark-label="cub" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cub：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cub：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cub" title="在有道查询 cub">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-080</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 幼兽</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rescue team cared for a lion <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cub</strong></a>, an elephant calf, a seal pup and an orphaned lamb.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援队照料一只幼狮、一头小象、一只小海豹和一只失去母亲的羔羊。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cub</strong></a>, calf, pup and lamb all depend on adult care early in life.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">幼兽、小牛类幼崽、小犬类幼崽和羔羊在生命早期都依赖成年动物照料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vet weighed each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cub</strong></a>, calf, pup and lamb before preparing its milk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">兽医给每只幼兽、小牛、小犬和羔羊称重后，再调配奶液。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a lion cub</span> · 幼狮</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cattle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cattle</strong></a> grazed while an ox pulled a cart, a bull stood nearby and a buffalo cooled in water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牛群吃草时，一头役牛拉车，一头公牛站在附近，一头水牛在水中纳凉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farm keeps <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cattle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cattle</strong></a>, uses an ox for work and houses its bull separately from the buffalo.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农场养牛，用役牛干活，并将公牛与水牛分开圈养。</p></li>

</ol>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 马</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horse</strong></a> wore a saddle, the donkey pulled in a harness, and the zebra grazed beyond the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马配着鞍，驴套着挽具拉车，斑马在篱笆外吃草。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the zebra, the trained <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horse</strong></a> accepted a saddle and the donkey tolerated a harness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与斑马不同，受训的马接受装鞍，驴也能适应挽具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper stored a saddle and harness near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horse</strong></a> and donkey enclosures, away from the zebra.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员把鞍和挽具存放在马、驴围栏附近，远离斑马。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a riding horse</span> · 骑乘马</p>

</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 斑马</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The horse wore a saddle, the donkey pulled in a harness, and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=zebra&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>zebra</strong></a> grazed beyond the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马配着鞍，驴套着挽具拉车，斑马在篱笆外吃草。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=zebra&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>zebra</strong></a>, the trained horse accepted a saddle and the donkey tolerated a harness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与斑马不同，受训的马接受装鞍，驴也能适应挽具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper stored a saddle and harness near the horse and donkey enclosures, away from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=zebra&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>zebra</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员把鞍和挽具存放在马、驴围栏附近，远离斑马。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">saddle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-091" aria-label="定位 saddle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-091" data-mark-label="saddle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="saddle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="saddle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saddle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 saddle" title="在有道查询 saddle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 鞍，马鞍；车座</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The horse wore a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saddle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saddle</strong></a>, the donkey pulled in a harness, and the zebra grazed beyond the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马配着鞍，驴套着挽具拉车，斑马在篱笆外吃草。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the zebra, the trained horse accepted a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saddle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saddle</strong></a> and the donkey tolerated a harness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与斑马不同，受训的马接受装鞍，驴也能适应挽具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper stored a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saddle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saddle</strong></a> and harness near the horse and donkey enclosures, away from the zebra.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员把鞍和挽具存放在马、驴围栏附近，远离斑马。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a leather saddle</span> · 皮马鞍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bridle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bridle</a></span> · 马勒</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hoof&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hoof</a></span> · 蹄</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-092">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the zebra, the trained horse accepted a saddle and the donkey tolerated a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harness</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与斑马不同，受训的马接受装鞍，驴也能适应挽具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper stored a saddle and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harness</strong></a> near the horse and donkey enclosures, away from the zebra.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员把鞍和挽具存放在马、驴围栏附近，远离斑马。</p></li>

### 词群 03.09｜鸟类、啮齿类与两栖动物 {#group-03-09}

**串联方式：** 先按猛禽和常见鸟类辨认，再切换到鼠、松鼠、野兔与青蛙，避免把它们当同一分类。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-093">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-093" class="vocabulary-word-title">falcon<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-093" aria-label="定位 falcon">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-093" data-mark-label="falcon" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="falcon：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="falcon：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=falcon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 falcon" title="在有道查询 falcon">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-093</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 隼，猎鹰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=falcon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>falcon</strong></a>, hawk, eagle and owl each uses sharp talons to capture prey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">隼、鹰、雕和猫头鹰都用锐利脚爪捕捉猎物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=falcon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>falcon</strong></a> and hawk hunted by day, while the owl emerged after the eagle returned to its nest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">隼和鹰白天捕猎，雕归巢后，猫头鹰才出来活动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rescuers released the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=falcon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>falcon</strong></a> and hawk but kept the injured eagle and owl for treatment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援人员放飞隼和鹰，但留下受伤的雕和猫头鹰治疗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a hunting falcon</span> · 捕猎中的隼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raptor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">raptor</a></span> · 猛禽</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=talon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">talon</a></span> · 猛禽利爪</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-094">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-094" class="vocabulary-word-title">hawk<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-094" aria-label="定位 hawk">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-094" data-mark-label="hawk" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hawk：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hawk：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hawk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hawk" title="在有道查询 hawk">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-094</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 鹰，隼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A falcon, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hawk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hawk</strong></a>, eagle and owl each uses sharp talons to capture prey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">隼、鹰、雕和猫头鹰都用锐利脚爪捕捉猎物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The falcon and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hawk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hawk</strong></a> hunted by day, while the owl emerged after the eagle returned to its nest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">隼和鹰白天捕猎，雕归巢后，猫头鹰才出来活动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rescuers released the falcon and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hawk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hawk</strong></a> but kept the injured eagle and owl for treatment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援人员放飞隼和鹰，但留下受伤的雕和猫头鹰治疗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a soaring hawk</span> · 翱翔的鹰</p>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The falcon and hawk hunted by day, while the owl emerged after the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eagle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eagle</strong></a> returned to its nest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">隼和鹰白天捕猎，雕归巢后，猫头鹰才出来活动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rescuers released the falcon and hawk but kept the injured <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eagle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eagle</strong></a> and owl for treatment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援人员放飞隼和鹰，但留下受伤的雕和猫头鹰治疗。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-096">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A falcon, hawk, eagle and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=owl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>owl</strong></a> each uses sharp talons to capture prey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">隼、鹰、雕和猫头鹰都用锐利脚爪捕捉猎物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The falcon and hawk hunted by day, while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=owl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>owl</strong></a> emerged after the eagle returned to its nest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">隼和鹰白天捕猎，雕归巢后，猫头鹰才出来活动。</p></li>
