<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">swallow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-097" aria-label="定位 swallow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-097" data-mark-label="swallow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="swallow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="swallow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 swallow" title="在有道查询 swallow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-097</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 燕子；v. 吞咽（第 12 章进食语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swallow</strong></a> flew over a sparrow, a pigeon and a crow feeding in the square.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">燕子飞过广场上觅食的麻雀、鸽子和乌鸦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swallow</strong></a> near the river, a sparrow in a hedge, a pigeon on a roof and a crow in a tree.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查记录了河边的燕子、篱笆中的麻雀、屋顶的鸽子和树上的乌鸦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the visiting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swallow</strong></a>, the sparrow, pigeon and crow remained through winter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与季节性到访的燕子不同，麻雀、鸽子和乌鸦整个冬天都留在当地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a migrating swallow</span> · 迁徙的燕子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=roost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">roost</a></span> · 栖息处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nestling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">nestling</a></span> · 雏鸟</li></ul>
</div>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 老鼠；卑鄙的人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A camera recorded a mouse, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rat</strong></a> and squirrel near the barn, a hare in the field and a frog by the pond.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">相机拍到谷仓附近的老鼠、大鼠和松鼠，以及田里的野兔和池塘边的青蛙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The flood displaced a mouse, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rat</strong></a>, a squirrel and a hare, while the frog moved into shallow water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪水使老鼠、大鼠、松鼠和野兔失去栖身处，青蛙则移入浅水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mouse and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rat</strong></a> ate grain, a squirrel carried nuts, a hare grazed and a frog caught insects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老鼠和大鼠吃谷粒，松鼠搬坚果，野兔吃草，青蛙捕虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a brown rat</span> · 褐家鼠</p>

### 词群 03.10｜动物怎样行动和发声 {#group-03-10}

**串联方式：** 用咬、蜇、吠、吼、摩擦、爬行等动作观察行为，把动作配给合适的动物。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">behaviour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-109" aria-label="定位 behaviour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-109" data-mark-label="behaviour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="behaviour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="behaviour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 behaviour" title="在有道查询 behaviour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">( = behavior )；n. 行为；活动方式</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Understanding animal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behaviour</strong></a> reduces the risk of a bite or sting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">理解动物行为可减少被咬或蜇的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper studied warning <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behaviour</strong></a> before approaching an animal likely to bite or sting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员在接近可能咬人或蜇人的动物前研究其警示行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A bite or sting may be defensive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behaviour</strong></a> rather than an attempt to hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咬或蜇可能是防御行为，而非捕猎行为。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">animal behaviour</span> · 动物行为</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aggression</a></span> · 攻击行为</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defence</a></span> · 防御</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-110">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">bite<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-110" aria-label="定位 bite">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-110" data-mark-label="bite" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bite：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bite：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bite" title="在有道查询 bite">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.咬；(昆虫)叮；吞饵；n. 咬伤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Understanding animal behaviour reduces the risk of a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bite</strong></a> or sting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">理解动物行为可减少被咬或蜇的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper studied warning behaviour before approaching an animal likely to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bite</strong></a> or sting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员在接近可能咬人或蜇人的动物前研究其警示行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bite</strong></a> or sting may be defensive behaviour rather than an attempt to hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咬或蜇可能是防御行为，而非捕猎行为。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a dog bite</span> · 狗咬伤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aggression</a></span> · 攻击行为</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defence</a></span> · 防御</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-111">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-111" class="vocabulary-word-title">sting<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-111" aria-label="定位 sting">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-111" data-mark-label="sting" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sting：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sting：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sting" title="在有道查询 sting">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-111</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 刺，叮；(使)感觉刺痛；n. 刺，螫针；蜇痛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Understanding animal behaviour reduces the risk of a bite or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sting</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">理解动物行为可减少被咬或蜇的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper studied warning behaviour before approaching an animal likely to bite or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sting</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员在接近可能咬人或蜇人的动物前研究其警示行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A bite or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sting</strong></a> may be defensive behaviour rather than an attempt to hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咬或蜇可能是防御行为，而非捕猎行为。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bee sting</span> · 蜂蜇伤</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-112">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-112" class="vocabulary-word-title">bark<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-112" aria-label="定位 bark">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-112" data-mark-label="bark" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bark：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bark：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bark" title="在有道查询 bark">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-112</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(狗等)吠；大声嚷；n. 树皮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We heard a dog <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bark</strong></a> and a lion roar, then watched a bear rub against a tree.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们听到狗吠和狮吼，随后看到熊蹭树。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Animals may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bark</strong></a> or roar to communicate, or rub against objects to leave scent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">动物可能通过吠叫或吼叫交流，也可能蹭物体留下气味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors heard the lion roar as the dogs began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bark</strong></a> and a nearby cat continued to rub against the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">狮子吼叫时狗开始吠叫，附近的猫继续蹭篱笆，游客听见了这些声音。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vocalisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">vocalisation</a></span> · 发声</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scent%20marking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">scent marking</a></span> · 气味标记</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-115">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cat began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=creep&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>creep</strong></a> closer as the beetle continued to crawl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">甲虫继续爬行时，猫开始悄悄靠近。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched a lizard crawl across the rock and a snake <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=creep&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>creep</strong></a> through the grass.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看蜥蜴爬过岩石，蛇在草丛中缓缓潜行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Hunters <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=creep&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>creep</strong></a> towards prey, while some small animals crawl into narrow shelters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">捕猎者悄悄接近猎物，一些小动物则爬进狭窄藏身处。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">creep through grass</span> · 在草丛中悄行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stealth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stealth</a></span> · 悄悄行动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=locomotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">locomotion</a></span> · 运动方式</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-116">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cat began to creep closer as the beetle continued to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crawl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crawl</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">甲虫继续爬行时，猫开始悄悄靠近。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched a lizard <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crawl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crawl</strong></a> across the rock and a snake creep through the grass.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看蜥蜴爬过岩石，蛇在草丛中缓缓潜行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Hunters creep towards prey, while some small animals <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crawl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crawl</strong></a> into narrow shelters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">捕猎者悄悄接近猎物，一些小动物则爬进狭窄藏身处。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">crawl along the ground</span> · 沿地爬行</p>

### 词群 03.11｜从天然栖息地到人工围养 {#group-03-11}

**串联方式：** 由栖息地进入巢与蜂房，再到笼、马厩、谷仓及围栏，比较开放与受限空间。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-117">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-117" class="vocabulary-word-title">habitat<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-117" aria-label="定位 habitat">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-117" data-mark-label="habitat" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="habitat：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="habitat：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=habitat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 habitat" title="在有道查询 habitat">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-117</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(动植物的)生活环境，栖息地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=habitat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>habitat</strong></a> survey recorded a bird's nest and a bee hive with an unusually large cell.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">栖息地调查记录了一个鸟巢和一个有异常大蜂房的蜂巢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=habitat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>habitat</strong></a> keeps the nest undisturbed and the hive intact, down to each wax cell.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护这一栖息地让鸟巢免受打扰，使蜂巢完整保存，直至每个蜡制蜂房。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hollow tree provides <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=habitat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>habitat</strong></a> for a nest or hive, whose bees build each cell from wax.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">树洞可为鸟巢或蜂巢提供栖息处，蜜蜂在蜂巢中用蜡建造每个蜂房。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">natural habitat</span> · 天然栖息地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shelter</a></span> · 庇护处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">colony</a></span> · 群体</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-118">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The habitat survey recorded a bird's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nest</strong></a> and a bee hive with an unusually large cell.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">栖息地调查记录了一个鸟巢和一个有异常大蜂房的蜂巢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting this habitat keeps the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nest</strong></a> undisturbed and the hive intact, down to each wax cell.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护这一栖息地让鸟巢免受打扰，使蜂巢完整保存，直至每个蜡制蜂房。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hollow tree provides habitat for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nest</strong></a> or hive, whose bees build each cell from wax.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">树洞可为鸟巢或蜂巢提供栖息处，蜜蜂在蜂巢中用蜡建造每个蜂房。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-119">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-119" class="vocabulary-word-title">hive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-119" aria-label="定位 hive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-119" data-mark-label="hive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hive" title="在有道查询 hive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-119</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 蜂房；繁忙的场所；v.(使)(蜂)入蜂箱；(像蜜蜂般)密集群居</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The habitat survey recorded a bird's nest and a bee <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hive</strong></a> with an unusually large cell.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">栖息地调查记录了一个鸟巢和一个有异常大蜂房的蜂巢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting this habitat keeps the nest undisturbed and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hive</strong></a> intact, down to each wax cell.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护这一栖息地让鸟巢免受打扰，使蜂巢完整保存，直至每个蜡制蜂房。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hollow tree provides habitat for a nest or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hive</strong></a>, whose bees build each cell from wax.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">树洞可为鸟巢或蜂巢提供栖息处，蜜蜂在蜂巢中用蜡建造每个蜂房。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bee hive</span> · 蜂巢</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shelter</a></span> · 庇护处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">colony</a></span> · 群体</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The habitat survey recorded a bird's nest and a bee hive with an unusually large <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cell</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">栖息地调查记录了一个鸟巢和一个有异常大蜂房的蜂巢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Protecting this habitat keeps the nest undisturbed and the hive intact, down to each wax <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cell</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">保护这一栖息地让鸟巢免受打扰，使蜂巢完整保存，直至每个蜡制蜂房。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hollow tree provides habitat for a nest or hive, whose bees build each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cell</strong></a> from wax.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">树洞可为鸟巢或蜂巢提供栖息处，蜜蜂在蜂巢中用蜡建造每个蜂房。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-121">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-121" class="vocabulary-word-title">cage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-121" aria-label="定位 cage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-121" data-mark-label="cage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cage" title="在有道查询 cage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-121</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 笼子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farm has a recovery <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cage</strong></a> near the stable and a large barn for cattle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农场在马厩附近设有康复笼，并有一个大型牛棚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers cleaned the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cage</strong></a>, repaired the stable door and replaced the barn roof.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人清理笼子、修理马厩门并更换牛棚屋顶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An injured bird rested in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cage</strong></a> while horses sheltered in the stable and cows entered the barn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">受伤的鸟在笼中休息，马在马厩躲避天气，牛则进入牛棚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an animal cage</span> · 兽笼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enclosure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">enclosure</a></span> · 围栏区</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=husbandry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">husbandry</a></span> · 畜牧业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers cleaned the cage, repaired the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stable</strong></a> door and replaced the barn roof.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人清理笼子、修理马厩门并更换牛棚屋顶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An injured bird rested in a cage while horses sheltered in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stable</strong></a> and cows entered the barn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">受伤的鸟在笼中休息，马在马厩躲避天气，牛则进入牛棚。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-124">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-124" class="vocabulary-word-title">hedge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-124" aria-label="定位 hedge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-124" data-mark-label="hedge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hedge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hedge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hedge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hedge" title="在有道查询 hedge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-124</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 树篱；障碍物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A broken bar left a gap in the barrier beside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hedge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hedge</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一根断裂的栏杆使树篱旁的屏障出现缺口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hedge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hedge</strong></a> forms a natural barrier beyond the metal bar at the gate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">树篱在门口金属横杆之外形成天然屏障。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper reinforced the barrier with another bar and trimmed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hedge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hedge</strong></a> for visibility.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员加装一根栏杆加固屏障，并修剪树篱以改善视野。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a dense hedge</span> · 浓密树篱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fence</a></span> · 围栏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boundary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">boundary</a></span> · 边界</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A broken bar left a gap in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barrier</strong></a> beside the hedge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一根断裂的栏杆使树篱旁的屏障出现缺口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The hedge forms a natural <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barrier</strong></a> beyond the metal bar at the gate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">树篱在门口金属横杆之外形成天然屏障。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper reinforced the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barrier</strong></a> with another bar and trimmed the hedge for visibility.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员加装一根栏杆加固屏障，并修剪树篱以改善视野。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-126">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-126" class="vocabulary-word-title">bar<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-126" aria-label="定位 bar">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-126" data-mark-label="bar" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bar：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bar：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bar" title="在有道查询 bar">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-126</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 横杆；栅条；酒吧（其他语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A broken <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bar</strong></a> left a gap in the barrier beside the hedge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一根断裂的栏杆使树篱旁的屏障出现缺口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The hedge forms a natural barrier beyond the metal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bar</strong></a> at the gate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">树篱在门口金属横杆之外形成天然屏障。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper reinforced the barrier with another <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bar</strong></a> and trimmed the hedge for visibility.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员加装一根栏杆加固屏障，并修剪树篱以改善视野。</p></li>
</ol>

</div>
</details>

### 词群 03.12｜从身体结构到生理和疾病 {#group-03-12}

**串联方式：** 把解剖、基因、代谢、营养与分泌排泄连接起来，再辨认病原体及流行病。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-127">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-127" class="vocabulary-word-title">anatomy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-127" aria-label="定位 anatomy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-127" data-mark-label="anatomy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="anatomy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="anatomy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anatomy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 anatomy" title="在有道查询 anatomy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-127</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 解剖学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team studied <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anatomy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anatomy</strong></a> and a resistance gene to understand survival during the epidemic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队研究了解剖结构和一个抗病基因，以理解动物为何在疫病流行时存活。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An epidemic prompted research into how one gene affects the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anatomy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anatomy</strong></a> of infected fish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一场疫病流行促使研究者探究某个基因如何影响受感染鱼的解剖结构。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Comparative <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anatomy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anatomy</strong></a> revealed tissue damage during the epidemic, while gene analysis suggested differences in resistance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比较解剖学揭示了疫病流行期间的组织损伤，基因分析则提示抗病能力存在差异。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">comparative anatomy</span> · 比较解剖学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=physiology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">physiology</a></span> · 生理学</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=immunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">immunity</a></span> · 免疫</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-128">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team studied anatomy and a resistance gene to understand survival during the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=epidemic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>epidemic</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队研究了解剖结构和一个抗病基因，以理解动物为何在疫病流行时存活。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=epidemic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>epidemic</strong></a> prompted research into how one gene affects the anatomy of infected fish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一场疫病流行促使研究者探究某个基因如何影响受感染鱼的解剖结构。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Comparative anatomy revealed tissue damage during the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=epidemic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>epidemic</strong></a>, while gene analysis suggested differences in resistance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比较解剖学揭示了疫病流行期间的组织损伤，基因分析则提示抗病能力存在差异。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-130">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 微生物，细菌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=germ&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>germ</strong></a> may be a virus or one of many bacteria, but not every microbe causes disease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">病原体可能是病毒或多种细菌之一，但并非每种微生物都致病。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The laboratory identified the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=germ&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>germ</strong></a> as a virus rather than bacteria or another type of microbe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验室确定该病原体是病毒，而非细菌或其他类型微生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students compared bacteria with a virus and discussed when a microbe is described as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=germ&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>germ</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生比较细菌和病毒，并讨论何时将微生物称为病原体。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a disease-causing germ</span> · 致病菌</p>

</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A germ may be a virus or one of many <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bacteria&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bacteria</strong></a>, but not every microbe causes disease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">病原体可能是病毒或多种细菌之一，但并非每种微生物都致病。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The laboratory identified the germ as a virus rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bacteria&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bacteria</strong></a> or another type of microbe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验室确定该病原体是病毒，而非细菌或其他类型微生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students compared <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bacteria&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bacteria</strong></a> with a virus and discussed when a microbe is described as a germ.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生比较细菌和病毒，并讨论何时将微生物称为病原体。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-133">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-133" class="vocabulary-word-title">microbe<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-133" aria-label="定位 microbe">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-133" data-mark-label="microbe" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="microbe：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="microbe：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microbe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 microbe" title="在有道查询 microbe">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-133</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 微生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A germ may be a virus or one of many bacteria, but not every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microbe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microbe</strong></a> causes disease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">病原体可能是病毒或多种细菌之一，但并非每种微生物都致病。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The laboratory identified the germ as a virus rather than bacteria or another type of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microbe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microbe</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验室确定该病原体是病毒，而非细菌或其他类型微生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students compared bacteria with a virus and discussed when a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microbe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microbe</strong></a> is described as a germ.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生比较细菌和病毒，并讨论何时将微生物称为病原体。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a soil microbe</span> · 土壤微生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pathogen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pathogen</a></span> · 病原体</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">infection</a></span> · 感染</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-134">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-134" class="vocabulary-word-title">metabolism<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-134" aria-label="定位 metabolism">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-134" data-mark-label="metabolism" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="metabolism：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="metabolism：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metabolism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 metabolism" title="在有道查询 metabolism">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-134</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 新陈代谢</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Healthy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metabolism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>metabolism</strong></a> requires protein and vitamin intake, while glands secrete substances and kidneys excrete waste.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">健康代谢需要蛋白质及维生素摄入，同时腺体分泌物质，肾脏排泄废物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The study examined how <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metabolism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>metabolism</strong></a> changes when animals lack a vitamin, digest protein, secrete hormones and excrete nitrogen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究考察动物缺乏维生素、消化蛋白质、分泌激素并排泄氮时，代谢如何变化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Animals use protein and vitamin resources in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metabolism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>metabolism</strong></a>, secrete digestive enzymes and excrete products they cannot use.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">动物在代谢中利用蛋白质和维生素，分泌消化酶并排泄无法利用的产物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">basal metabolism</span> · 基础代谢</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digestion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">digestion</a></span> · 消化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nutrition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">nutrition</a></span> · 营养</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 03.13｜觅食、本能与适应 {#group-03-13}

**串联方式：** 从吞食和本能进入智力、感官和灵活性，再看夜行、休眠与冬眠。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-139">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-139" class="vocabulary-word-title">devour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-139" aria-label="定位 devour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-139" data-mark-label="devour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="devour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="devour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 devour" title="在有道查询 devour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-139</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 吞食，狼吞虎咽地吃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cat's instinct to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devour</strong></a> prey looked like an intuitive response rather than a learned decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">猫吞食猎物的本能看似直觉反应，而非习得的决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An intuitive explanation is that hunger triggers the instinct to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devour</strong></a> available food.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一种直观解释是，饥饿触发吞食现有食物的本能。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her intuitive understanding of the animal's hunting instinct helped predict when it would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devour</strong></a> its prey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她对该动物捕猎本能的直觉理解，帮助她预测它何时会吞食猎物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">devour prey</span> · 吞食猎物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instinctive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">instinctive</a></span> · 本能的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appetite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appetite</a></span> · 食欲</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-140">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-140" class="vocabulary-word-title">instinct<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-140" aria-label="定位 instinct">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-140" data-mark-label="instinct" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="instinct：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="instinct：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instinct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 instinct" title="在有道查询 instinct">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-140</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 本能，天性；直觉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cat's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instinct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>instinct</strong></a> to devour prey looked like an intuitive response rather than a learned decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">猫吞食猎物的本能看似直觉反应，而非习得的决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An intuitive explanation is that hunger triggers the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instinct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>instinct</strong></a> to devour available food.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一种直观解释是，饥饿触发吞食现有食物的本能。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her intuitive understanding of the animal's hunting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instinct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>instinct</strong></a> helped predict when it would devour its prey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她对该动物捕猎本能的直觉理解，帮助她预测它何时会吞食猎物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">survival instinct</span> · 求生本能</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instinctive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">instinctive</a></span> · 本能的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appetite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appetite</a></span> · 食欲</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-141">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-141" class="vocabulary-word-title">intuitive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-141" aria-label="定位 intuitive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-141" data-mark-label="intuitive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="intuitive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="intuitive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intuitive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 intuitive" title="在有道查询 intuitive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-141</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 直觉的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cat's instinct to devour prey looked like an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intuitive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intuitive</strong></a> response rather than a learned decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">猫吞食猎物的本能看似直觉反应，而非习得的决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intuitive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intuitive</strong></a> explanation is that hunger triggers the instinct to devour available food.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一种直观解释是，饥饿触发吞食现有食物的本能。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intuitive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intuitive</strong></a> understanding of the animal's hunting instinct helped predict when it would devour its prey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她对该动物捕猎本能的直觉理解，帮助她预测它何时会吞食猎物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an intuitive judgement</span> · 直觉判断</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instinctive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">instinctive</a></span> · 本能的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appetite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appetite</a></span> · 食欲</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-142">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-142" class="vocabulary-word-title">potential<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-142" aria-label="定位 potential">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-142" data-mark-label="potential" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="potential：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="potential：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=potential&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 potential" title="在有道查询 potential">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-142</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 潜力；adj. 潜在的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The robot models animal intelligence with sensitive sensors, flexible joints and a functional design that has considerable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=potential&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>potential</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器人用灵敏传感器、灵活关节和有实用功能的设计模拟动物智能，具有相当大潜力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers assessed the animal's intelligence and learning <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=potential&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>potential</strong></a> alongside its sensitive hearing, flexible movements and functional vision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员评估动物的智力和学习潜力，以及其灵敏听觉、灵活动作和正常视觉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flexible training programme may reveal the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=potential&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>potential</strong></a> intelligence of an animal with sensitive hearing but only one functional eye.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">灵活的训练方案可能揭示一只听觉灵敏但仅一只眼睛有功能的动物的智力潜力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">learning potential</span> · 学习潜力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cognition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cognition</a></span> · 认知</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptability</a></span> · 适应能力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The robot models animal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intelligence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intelligence</strong></a> with sensitive sensors, flexible joints and a functional design that has considerable potential.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器人用灵敏传感器、灵活关节和有实用功能的设计模拟动物智能，具有相当大潜力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers assessed the animal's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intelligence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intelligence</strong></a> and learning potential alongside its sensitive hearing, flexible movements and functional vision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员评估动物的智力和学习潜力，以及其灵敏听觉、灵活动作和正常视觉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flexible training programme may reveal the potential <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intelligence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intelligence</strong></a> of an animal with sensitive hearing but only one functional eye.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">灵活的训练方案可能揭示一只听觉灵敏但仅一只眼睛有功能的动物的智力潜力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">animal intelligence</span> · 动物智力</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The robot models animal intelligence with sensitive sensors, flexible joints and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=functional&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>functional</strong></a> design that has considerable potential.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器人用灵敏传感器、灵活关节和有实用功能的设计模拟动物智能，具有相当大潜力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers assessed the animal's intelligence and learning potential alongside its sensitive hearing, flexible movements and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=functional&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>functional</strong></a> vision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员评估动物的智力和学习潜力，以及其灵敏听觉、灵活动作和正常视觉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flexible training programme may reveal the potential intelligence of an animal with sensitive hearing but only one <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=functional&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>functional</strong></a> eye.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">灵活的训练方案可能揭示一只听觉灵敏但仅一只眼睛有功能的动物的智力潜力。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cognition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cognition</a></span> · 认知</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptability</a></span> · 适应能力</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-147">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-147" class="vocabulary-word-title">acoustic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-147" aria-label="定位 acoustic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-147" data-mark-label="acoustic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="acoustic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="acoustic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acoustic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 acoustic" title="在有道查询 acoustic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-147</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 听觉的；声音的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acoustic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Acoustic</strong></a> and optical sensors monitored a nocturnal bat as it became dormant during hibernation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">声学和光学传感器监测一只夜行蝙蝠在冬眠期间进入休眠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The nocturnal animal produced fewer <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acoustic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>acoustic</strong></a> signals and remained invisible to optical surveys while dormant in hibernation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">夜行动物冬眠时处于休眠状态，产生更少声学信号，也未出现在光学调查中。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers combined <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acoustic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>acoustic</strong></a> recording with optical imaging to distinguish normal nocturnal rest from a dormant period of hibernation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员结合录音和光学成像，以区分正常夜行作息中的休息与冬眠休眠期。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">acoustic signals</span> · 声学信号</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=echolocation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">echolocation</a></span> · 回声定位</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=torpor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">torpor</a></span> · 蛰伏</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Acoustic and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=optical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>optical</strong></a> sensors monitored a nocturnal bat as it became dormant during hibernation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">声学和光学传感器监测一只夜行蝙蝠在冬眠期间进入休眠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The nocturnal animal produced fewer acoustic signals and remained invisible to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=optical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>optical</strong></a> surveys while dormant in hibernation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">夜行动物冬眠时处于休眠状态，产生更少声学信号，也未出现在光学调查中。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers combined acoustic recording with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=optical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>optical</strong></a> imaging to distinguish normal nocturnal rest from a dormant period of hibernation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员结合录音和光学成像，以区分正常夜行作息中的休息与冬眠休眠期。</p></li>
