### 词群 03.14｜追踪、捕食与保护 {#group-03-14}

**串联方式：** 追踪动物足迹，理解猎物与捕食者，再讨论脆弱性、生存、圈养、驯养和照料；alternate 单独记“交替”。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-152">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-152" class="vocabulary-word-title">track<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-152" aria-label="定位 track">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-152" data-mark-label="track" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="track：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="track：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=track&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 track" title="在有道查询 track">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-152</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 追踪；n. 足迹，踪迹；轨道</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=track&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>track</strong></a> and a trace of fur before taking an alternate route around the marsh.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们循着足迹和一丝毛发前进，随后改走另一条路绕过沼泽。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An alternate trail allowed researchers to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=track&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>track</strong></a> the wolf without leaving a trace near its den.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">另一条小径使研究人员能追踪狼，而不在狼穴附近留下痕迹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=track&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>track</strong></a> disappears, search for another trace and consider an alternate path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若足迹消失，寻找其他痕迹，并考虑替代路径。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">track an animal</span> · 追踪动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=footprint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">footprint</a></span> · 脚印</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tracking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tracking</a></span> · 追踪</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-153">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-153" class="vocabulary-word-title">trace<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-153" aria-label="定位 trace">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-153" data-mark-label="trace" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trace：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trace：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trace" title="在有道查询 trace">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-153</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 追踪；查出；追溯；n. 痕迹；少许</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed a track and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trace</strong></a> of fur before taking an alternate route around the marsh.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们循着足迹和一丝毛发前进，随后改走另一条路绕过沼泽。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An alternate trail allowed researchers to track the wolf without leaving a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trace</strong></a> near its den.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">另一条小径使研究人员能追踪狼，而不在狼穴附近留下痕迹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If the track disappears, search for another <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trace</strong></a> and consider an alternate path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若足迹消失，寻找其他痕迹，并考虑替代路径。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a trace of fur</span> · 一丝毛发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=footprint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">footprint</a></span> · 脚印</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tracking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tracking</a></span> · 追踪</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-154">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-154" class="vocabulary-word-title">alternate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-154" aria-label="定位 alternate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-154" data-mark-label="alternate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="alternate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="alternate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alternate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 alternate" title="在有道查询 alternate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-154</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)交替，(使)轮流</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed a track and a trace of fur before taking an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alternate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>alternate</strong></a> route around the marsh.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们循着足迹和一丝毛发前进，随后改走另一条路绕过沼泽。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alternate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>alternate</strong></a> trail allowed researchers to track the wolf without leaving a trace near its den.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">另一条小径使研究人员能追踪狼，而不在狼穴附近留下痕迹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If the track disappears, search for another trace and consider an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alternate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>alternate</strong></a> path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若足迹消失，寻找其他痕迹，并考虑替代路径。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">alternate routes</span> · 替代路线</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=footprint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">footprint</a></span> · 脚印</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tracking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tracking</a></span> · 追踪</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-155">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-155" class="vocabulary-word-title">prey<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-155" aria-label="定位 prey">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-155" data-mark-label="prey" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prey：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prey：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prey" title="在有道查询 prey">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-155</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 被捕食的动物，猎物；v. 捕食</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A captive predator may still hunt <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prey</strong></a>, while its potential victim shows defensive behaviour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">圈养捕食者仍可能捕猎，潜在受害动物则表现出防御行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prey</strong></a> adopted a defensive posture, and the captive predator failed to catch its intended victim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">猎物采取防御姿势，圈养捕食者未能抓住目标猎物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared defensive responses in wild <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prey</strong></a> and a captive animal that had survived as a predator's victim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较野生猎物与一只曾遭捕食者攻击而幸存的圈养动物的防御反应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hunt prey</span> · 捕猎猎物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">predation</a></span> · 捕食</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defence</a></span> · 防御</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-156">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-156" class="vocabulary-word-title">predator<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-156" aria-label="定位 predator">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-156" data-mark-label="predator" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="predator：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="predator：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 predator" title="在有道查询 predator">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-156</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 掠夺者；捕食性动物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A captive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>predator</strong></a> may still hunt prey, while its potential victim shows defensive behaviour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">圈养捕食者仍可能捕猎，潜在受害动物则表现出防御行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The prey adopted a defensive posture, and the captive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>predator</strong></a> failed to catch its intended victim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">猎物采取防御姿势，圈养捕食者未能抓住目标猎物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared defensive responses in wild prey and a captive animal that had survived as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>predator</strong></a>'s victim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较野生猎物与一只曾遭捕食者攻击而幸存的圈养动物的防御反应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an apex predator</span> · 顶级捕食者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">predation</a></span> · 捕食</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defence</a></span> · 防御</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A captive predator may still hunt prey, while its potential <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>victim</strong></a> shows defensive behaviour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">圈养捕食者仍可能捕猎，潜在受害动物则表现出防御行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The prey adopted a defensive posture, and the captive predator failed to catch its intended <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>victim</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">猎物采取防御姿势，圈养捕食者未能抓住目标猎物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared defensive responses in wild prey and a captive animal that had survived as a predator's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>victim</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较野生猎物与一只曾遭捕食者攻击而幸存的圈养动物的防御反应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a helpless victim</span> · 无助的受害者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=predation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">predation</a></span> · 捕食</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defence</a></span> · 防御</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A captive predator may still hunt prey, while its potential victim shows <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defensive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defensive</strong></a> behaviour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">圈养捕食者仍可能捕猎，潜在受害动物则表现出防御行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The prey adopted a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defensive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defensive</strong></a> posture, and the captive predator failed to catch its intended victim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">猎物采取防御姿势，圈养捕食者未能抓住目标猎物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defensive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defensive</strong></a> responses in wild prey and a captive animal that had survived as a predator's victim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较野生猎物与一只曾遭捕食者攻击而幸存的圈养动物的防御反应。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-160">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-160" class="vocabulary-word-title">undergo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-160" aria-label="定位 undergo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-160" data-mark-label="undergo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="undergo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="undergo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undergo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 undergo" title="在有道查询 undergo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-160</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 经历，经受</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Vulnerable species that exist near subsistence settlements may suffer disturbance and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undergo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undergo</strong></a> rapid population decline.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在维持基本生计的聚居地附近生存的脆弱物种，可能受到干扰并经历种群迅速下降。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When subsistence hunters suffer food shortages, vulnerable animals may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undergo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undergo</strong></a> greater pressure and struggle to exist nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">维生猎人遭遇食物短缺时，脆弱动物可能承受更大压力，难以在附近生存。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Communities that exist through subsistence fishing can suffer when vulnerable fish populations <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undergo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undergo</strong></a> collapse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">依靠维生捕鱼生存的社区，会在脆弱鱼类种群崩溃时遭受损失。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">undergo treatment</span> · 接受治疗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=livelihood&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">livelihood</a></span> · 生计</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vulnerability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">vulnerability</a></span> · 脆弱性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-161">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 遭受；受折磨；变糟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Vulnerable species that exist near subsistence settlements may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suffer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suffer</strong></a> disturbance and undergo rapid population decline.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在维持基本生计的聚居地附近生存的脆弱物种，可能受到干扰并经历种群迅速下降。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When subsistence hunters <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suffer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suffer</strong></a> food shortages, vulnerable animals may undergo greater pressure and struggle to exist nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">维生猎人遭遇食物短缺时，脆弱动物可能承受更大压力，难以在附近生存。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Communities that exist through subsistence fishing can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suffer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suffer</strong></a> when vulnerable fish populations undergo collapse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">依靠维生捕鱼生存的社区，会在脆弱鱼类种群崩溃时遭受损失。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When subsistence hunters suffer food shortages, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vulnerable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vulnerable</strong></a> animals may undergo greater pressure and struggle to exist nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">维生猎人遭遇食物短缺时，脆弱动物可能承受更大压力，难以在附近生存。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Communities that exist through subsistence fishing can suffer when <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vulnerable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vulnerable</strong></a> fish populations undergo collapse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">依靠维生捕鱼生存的社区，会在脆弱鱼类种群崩溃时遭受损失。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=livelihood&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">livelihood</a></span> · 生计</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vulnerability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">vulnerability</a></span> · 脆弱性</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsistence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subsistence</strong></a> hunters suffer food shortages, vulnerable animals may undergo greater pressure and struggle to exist nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">维生猎人遭遇食物短缺时，脆弱动物可能承受更大压力，难以在附近生存。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Communities that exist through <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsistence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subsistence</strong></a> fishing can suffer when vulnerable fish populations undergo collapse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">依靠维生捕鱼生存的社区，会在脆弱鱼类种群崩溃时遭受损失。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="03-165">
<summary><h4 id="word-03-165" class="vocabulary-word-title">exterminate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-03-165" aria-label="定位 exterminate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="03-165" data-mark-label="exterminate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="exterminate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="exterminate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exterminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 exterminate" title="在有道查询 exterminate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">03-165</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 消灭，根除</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper and shepherd opposed plans to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exterminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exterminate</strong></a> wolves but agreed not to tame wild cubs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员和牧羊人反对灭绝狼群的计划，但同意不去驯服野生幼狼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A shepherd asked the keeper how to tame a rescued dog without trying to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exterminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exterminate</strong></a> nearby predators.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牧羊人询问饲养员，如何驯服获救犬，同时避免试图消灭附近捕食者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper helped the shepherd tame a frightened horse while specialists worked to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exterminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exterminate</strong></a> an invasive pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员帮助牧羊人驯服受惊的马，专家们则努力消灭一种入侵害虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">exterminate pests</span> · 消灭害虫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=domestication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">domestication</a></span> · 驯化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stewardship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stewardship</a></span> · 管护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 驯养；制服；adj. 驯服的；乏味的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper and shepherd opposed plans to exterminate wolves but agreed not to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tame</strong></a> wild cubs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员和牧羊人反对灭绝狼群的计划，但同意不去驯服野生幼狼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A shepherd asked the keeper how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tame</strong></a> a rescued dog without trying to exterminate nearby predators.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牧羊人询问饲养员，如何驯服获救犬，同时避免试图消灭附近捕食者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper helped the shepherd <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tame</strong></a> a frightened horse while specialists worked to exterminate an invasive pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员帮助牧羊人驯服受惊的马，专家们则努力消灭一种入侵害虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tame a horse</span> · 驯服马匹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=domestication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">domestication</a></span> · 驯化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stewardship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stewardship</a></span> · 管护</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=keeper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>keeper</strong></a> helped the shepherd tame a frightened horse while specialists worked to exterminate an invasive pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员帮助牧羊人驯服受惊的马，专家们则努力消灭一种入侵害虫。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shepherd&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shepherd</strong></a> opposed plans to exterminate wolves but agreed not to tame wild cubs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员和牧羊人反对灭绝狼群的计划，但同意不去驯服野生幼狼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shepherd&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shepherd</strong></a> asked the keeper how to tame a rescued dog without trying to exterminate nearby predators.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牧羊人询问饲养员，如何驯服获救犬，同时避免试图消灭附近捕食者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The keeper helped the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shepherd&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shepherd</strong></a> tame a frightened horse while specialists worked to exterminate an invasive pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饲养员帮助牧羊人驯服受惊的马，专家们则努力消灭一种入侵害虫。</p></li>
</ol>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **a natural habitat**：自然栖息地。
- **predators and prey**：捕食者与猎物。
- **be vulnerable to disease**：容易受到疾病侵害。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

Each species has features that help it survive in its habitat. A bird uses its beak to feed and its wings to fly, while some mammals rely on fur for warmth. Predators hunt prey, and both need suitable living conditions. Habitat loss can leave wildlife vulnerable.

**中文理解：** 每个物种都有帮助它在栖息地生存的特征。鸟用喙进食、用翅膀飞行，一些哺乳动物靠皮毛保暖。捕食者猎取猎物，双方都需要适宜的生存条件。栖息地丧失会使野生动物变得脆弱。
